# MySQL Percona Operator

[![Artifact Hub](https://img.shields.io/endpoint?url=https://artifacthub.io/badge/repository/mysql-percona-operator)](https://artifacthub.io/packages/search?repo=mysql-percona-operator)

Helm chart for rendering Percona XtraDB Cluster Operator custom resources from declarative values.

The chart does not install Percona operator CRDs or the operator deployment. It only renders `PerconaXtraDBCluster`, `PerconaXtraDBClusterBackup`, and `PerconaXtraDBClusterRestore` objects for clusters where the `pxc.percona.com` CRDs already exist.

## Quick Start

Install the upstream Percona CRDs first, or deploy the full operator bundle separately:

```bash
kubectl apply --server-side -f https://raw.githubusercontent.com/percona/percona-xtradb-cluster-operator/main/deploy/crd.yaml
```

Install the chart from the repository root:

```bash
helm install mysql-percona-operator . \
  --namespace databases \
  --create-namespace \
  -f values.yaml.example
```

Install the local README generator hook:

```bash
pre-commit install
pre-commit install-hooks
```

## Supported Resources

The chart can render these Percona custom resource kinds:

- `PerconaXtraDBCluster`
- `PerconaXtraDBClusterBackup`
- `PerconaXtraDBClusterRestore`

The resources reconcile only when the Percona operator/controller is installed in the target cluster.

## Values Model

Each top-level map in [values.yaml](values.yaml) maps resource names to one resource kind:

- `perconaXtraDBClusters`
- `perconaXtraDBClusterBackups`
- `perconaXtraDBClusterRestores`

Every map entry uses the same generic contract:

| Field | Required | Description |
|-------|----------|-------------|
| map key | yes | Resource name used for `metadata.name`. |
| `namespace` | no | Namespace for namespaced resources. Defaults to the Helm release namespace. |
| `finalizers` | no | Optional `metadata.finalizers` list. |
| `labels` | no | Labels merged on top of built-in chart labels and `commonLabels`. |
| `annotations` | no | Annotations merged on top of `commonAnnotations`. |
| `apiVersion` | no | Per-resource API version override. |
| `spec` | no | Raw resource spec rendered as-is. |
| `status` | no | Optional raw status block. Usually not managed through Helm in production. |

In a higher-precedence values file, set a map entry to `null` to suppress a default resource from a lower-precedence values file.

Global controls:

- `enabled`
- `global` (accepted for umbrella-chart compatibility and ignored by this chart)
- `nameOverride`
- `commonLabels`
- `commonAnnotations`
- `apiVersions.*`

The value contract is validated by [values.schema.json](values.schema.json).

## Helm Values

This section is generated from [values.yaml](values.yaml) by `helm-docs`. Edit [values.yaml](values.yaml) comments or [docs/README.md.gotmpl](docs/README.md.gotmpl), then run `pre-commit run helm-docs --all-files` or `make docs` if you need to refresh it outside a commit.

`values.yaml` contains the reserved `__helm_docs_example__` resource entry for each collection. It is ignored at render time and exists only so `helm-docs` can document nested CRD fields sourced from the upstream Percona schemas.

## Included Values Files

- [values.yaml](values.yaml): minimal defaults that render no resources.
- [values.yaml.example](values.yaml.example): complete example covering cluster, backup, and restore resources with consistent cross-references.

Use [values.yaml.example](values.yaml.example) as a starting point and remove the resource-name keys you do not need.

## Testing

The repository uses three test layers:

- `tests/units/` for `helm-unittest` suites and backward compatibility checks
- `tests/e2e/` for local kind-based Helm install checks against real Percona CRDs
- `tests/smokes/` for render and schema smoke scenarios

Representative local commands:

```bash
helm lint . -f values.yaml.example
helm template mysql-percona-operator . -f values.yaml.example
helm unittest -f 'tests/units/*_test.yaml' .
sh tests/units/backward_compatibility_test.sh
python3 tests/smokes/run/smoke.py --scenario example-render
make test-e2e
```

Detailed test documentation is available in [docs/TESTS.MD](docs/TESTS.MD).

Local setup instructions for the development and test toolchain are available in [docs/DEPENDENCY.md](docs/DEPENDENCY.md).

The `e2e` layer is intentionally kept out of GitLab CI and is expected to be run locally through [Makefile](Makefile) or directly via `tests/e2e/test-e2e.sh`.

## Notes

- Install the upstream Percona CRDs before using the chart in a real cluster.
- Reconciliation requires the Percona operator/controller; this chart only renders CRs.
- Public `kubeconform` schema catalogs for `pxc.percona.com` can lag behind the upstream Percona CRD bundle. The example values intentionally avoid known catalog-incompatible fields such as `spec.backup.schedule[].retention`.
- The `kubeconform` smoke scenario still skips `pxc.percona.com` kinds by default because the public catalog should not be treated as the source of truth for Percona CR validation. Real API validation is covered by the e2e runner after it applies the upstream CRDs from `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/percona/percona-xtradb-cluster-operator/main/deploy/crd.yaml`.

## Repository Layout

| Path | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| [Chart.yaml](Chart.yaml) | Chart metadata. |
| [values.yaml](values.yaml) | Minimal default values and `helm-docs` source comments. |
| [docs/README.md.gotmpl](docs/README.md.gotmpl) | Template used by `helm-docs` to build `README.md`. |
| [.pre-commit-config.yaml](.pre-commit-config.yaml) | Local hooks, including automatic `README.md` generation on commit. |
| [values.yaml.example](values.yaml.example) | Full example configuration. |
| [values.schema.json](values.schema.json) | JSON schema for chart values. |
| [templates/](templates) | One template per supported Percona kind plus shared helpers. |
| [tests/units/](tests/units) | Compact Helm unit suites and backward compatibility checks. |
| [tests/e2e/](tests/e2e) | kind-based end-to-end installation checks. |
| [tests/smokes/](tests/smokes) | Smoke scenarios for render and schema validation. |
| [docs/DEPENDENCY.md](docs/DEPENDENCY.md) | Local dependency installation guide for development and tests. |
| [docs/TESTS.MD](docs/TESTS.MD) | Detailed testing documentation. |
